-% --- Test ---

-%            charlie investit 2000 (alice+bob recoivent 1000 chacun).

+get_bal(A, B, C) :-
+    loop_aux_1(B, A, 0, C).
+set_bal(A, B, C, D) :-
+    loop_aux_2(C, A, B, D).
+transfer(A, B, C, D, E) :-
+    get_bal(A, D, F),
+    G is F,
+    H is G-C,
+    I is H,
+    set_bal(A, I, D, J),
+    get_bal(B, J, K),
+    L is K,
+    M is L+C,
+    N is M,
+    set_bal(B, N, J, E).
+init(A, _, state([A])).
+invest(A, B, state(C), D, E, F) :-
+    aux_cond_1(B, A, E, C, F, D, state(C)).
+aux_cond_1(A, B, C, D, E, state([B|D]), _) :-
+    A>=1000,
+    !,
+    transfer(B, this, A, C, F),
+    loop_aux_3(D, 0, G),
+    H is A//G,
+    I is H,
+    loop_aux_4(D, I, F, E).
+aux_cond_1(_, _, A, _, A, B, B).
+goal([A, B, C, D]) :-
+    init(alice, _, E),

+           E,
+           F,

+    C is K,
+    get_bal(this, H, L),
+    D is L.
+loop_aux_1([], _, A, A).
+loop_aux_1([bal(A, B)|C], D, E, F) :-
+    aux_cond_2(A, D, G, B, E),
+    loop_aux_1(C, D, G, F).
+aux_cond_2(A, A, B, B, _) :-
+    !.
+aux_cond_2(_, _, A, _, A).
+loop_aux_2([], _, _, []).
+loop_aux_2([bal(A, B)|C], D, E, F) :-
+    aux_cond_3(A, D, F, E, G, B),

+aux_cond_3(A, A, [bal(A, B)|C], B, C, _) :-
+    !.
+aux_cond_3(A, _, [bal(A, B)|C], _, C, B).
+loop_aux_3([], A, A).
+loop_aux_3([_|A], B, C) :-
+    D is B+1,
+    loop_aux_3(A, D, C).
+loop_aux_4([], _, A, A).
+loop_aux_4([A|B], C, D, E) :-
+    transfer(this, A, C, D, F),
+    loop_aux_4(B, C, F, E).
